The submission is rejected and the reader is told why.\n\n## Trigger\n\nA reader submits `POST /books` where the `title` form field is missing, empty, or whitespace-only.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. `POST /books` receives form fields `title` and `author`.\n2. The title is trimmed. If the trimmed title is empty, validation fails. Title is checked before author, so when both are empty the rejection names `title`.\n3. The route answers status 400 and stores nothing.\n4. If the request carries `Accept: application/json`, the body is JSON with `error` (a non-empty reason string) and `field` equal to `title`.\n5. Otherwise the page is re-rendered in the shared layout's error region with the reason shown, still with status 400.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `POST /books` | Extended by this feature with title validation; valid submissions remain owned by FEATURE-AddBook.md |\n\n## Reads\n\nNone.\n\n## Writes\n\nNone. Error region: shows the rejection reason when a submission was rejected; empty otherwise.\n2. Add form: text inputs `title` and `author`, and a submit button, posting to `POST /books`.\n3. Book list: an ordered list of books in insertion order. Each entry shows title and author and a remove form posting to `POST /books/{id}/delete`.\n4. When no books exist the list area shows an empty-state message.\n\n## Interactions\n\n| Interaction | Route | Result |\n|-------------|-------|--------|\n| Open the page | `GET /` | Page lists every stored book in the order added |\n| Submit the add form | `POST /books` | Valid: 303 redirect back to `/`. Rejected: 400 and the page re-rendered with the reason |\n| Press remove on a book | `POST /books/{id}/delete` | 303 redirect back to `/`; the book is gone on the next read |\n| Read the list as data | `GET /books` | JSON array of `id`, `title`, `author` in insertion order |\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-AddBook.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC screen-index-reachable ===\nIntent: The reading list page at GET / answers 200 and GET /books answers 200 with a JSON array.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=pytest; is rejected with a reported reason and nothing is stored. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-AddBook.md, UI-GENERAL.md |\n| Provides | None |\n| Consumes | POST /books |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nThe reader must never be able to add a book without a title. The submission is rejected and the reader is told why.\n\n## Trigger\n\nA reader submits `POST /books` where the `title` form field is missing, empty, or whitespace-only.\n\n## Sequence\n\n1. `POST /books` receives form fields `title` and `author`.\n2. The title is trimmed. If the trimmed title is empty, validation fails. Title is checked before author, so when both are empty the rejection names `title`.\n3. The route answers status 400 and stores nothing.\n4. If the request carries `Accept: application/json`, the body is JSON with `error` (a non-empty reason string) and `field` equal to `title`.\n5. Otherwise the page is re-rendered in the shared layout's error region with the reason shown, still with status 400.\n\n## Routes\n\n| Route | Behavior |\n|-------|----------|\n| `POST /books` | Extended by this feature with title validation; valid submissions remain owned by FEATURE-AddBook.md |\n\n## Reads\n\nNone.\n\n## Writes\n\nNone. A rejected submission writes nothing.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by FEATURE-AddBook.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC reject-title-empty-json ===\nIntent: An empty title on POST /books answers 400 with a non-empty reason naming the title field and stores nothing.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: python-package=pytest;
